  <script>
    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    let library = { groups: [], items: [] };
    let currentPath = [];
    let openItemId = null;
    let replayState = '';
    let libraryLoaded = false;
    let toastTimer;

    async function api(url, options = {}) {
      let response;
      try { response = await fetch(url, options); }
      catch { throw new Error('Can’t reach Archive Shelf’s local server. Close the old server window, then start Archive Shelf with its updated launcher.'); }
      let payload;
      try { payload = await response.json(); }
      catch { throw new Error('The old file server is still running. Close its black window, then start Archive Shelf with its updated launcher.'); }
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(payload.error || 'Something went wrong.');
      return payload;
    }
    function normalized(path) { return String(path || '').split(/[\\/]/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean); }
    function pathString(parts) { return parts.join(' / '); }
    function resolveMoveDestination(value, baseParts = currentPath) {
      const trimmed = String(value || '').trim();
      if (!trimmed) return [];
      let explicitRoot = false;
      let pathText = trimmed;
      const rootPrefix = pathText.match(/^all\s+folders\s*(?:\/|\\|>)\s*/i);
      if (rootPrefix) { explicitRoot = true; pathText = pathText.slice(rootPrefix[0].length); }
      else if (/^[\\/]/.test(pathText)) { explicitRoot = true; pathText = pathText.replace(/^[\\/]+/, ''); }
      const typed = normalized(pathText);
      if (!typed.length) return [];
      const knownPaths = [...library.groups, ...library.items.map(item => item.folder || '')]
        .map(path => normalized(String(path || '')))
        .filter(parts => parts.length);
      const findKnown = parts => {
        const wanted = pathString(parts).toLocaleLowerCase();
        const found = knownPaths.find(candidate => pathString(candidate).toLocaleLowerCase() === wanted);
        return found ? [...found] : null;
      };
      if (explicitRoot) return findKnown(typed) || typed;
      const base = normalized(pathString(baseParts));
      const startsWithBase = base.length <= typed.length && base.every((part, index) => part.toLocaleLowerCase() === typed[index].toLocaleLowerCase());
      if (startsWithBase) return findKnown(typed) || typed;
      const relativePath = [...base, ...typed];
      const relativeMatch = findKnown(relativePath);
      if (relativeMatch) return relativeMatch;
      const fullMatch = findKnown(typed);
      if (fullMatch) return fullMatch;
      return relativePath;
    }
    function cleanReplayState(value) {
      if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 12000) return '';
      const incoming = new URLSearchParams(value.startsWith('#') ? value.slice(1) : value);
      const result = new URLSearchParams();
      for (const key of ['url', 'ts', 'query', 'view']) if (incoming.has(key)) result.set(key, incoming.get(key) || '');
      return result.toString();
    }
    function readRoute() {
      const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
      const archive = params.get('archive') || '';
      const validArchiveId = /^[a-f0-9]{32}$/i.test(archive);
      return {
        folder: normalized(params.get('folder') || ''),
        archiveId: validArchiveId ? archive.toLowerCase() : null,
        replayState: validArchiveId ? cleanReplayState(params.get('replay') || '') : ''
      };
    }
    function writeRoute(mode = 'push') {
      const url = new URL(location.href);
      const folder = pathString(currentPath);
      if (folder) url.searchParams.set('folder', folder); else url.searchParams.delete('folder');
      if (openItemId) url.searchParams.set('archive', openItemId); else url.searchParams.delete('archive');
      if (openItemId && replayState) url.searchParams.set('replay', replayState); else url.searchParams.delete('replay');
      const method = mode === 'replace' ? 'replaceState' : 'pushState';
      if (mode === 'push' && url.href === location.href) return;
      history[method]({ archiveShelf: true, folder: [...currentPath], archiveId: openItemId, replayState }, '', url);
    }
    function navigate(folder, archiveId = null) {
      currentPath = [...folder];
      openItemId = archiveId;
      replayState = '';
      writeRoute('push');
      render();
    }
    function isInside(candidate, parent) { return candidate === parent || candidate.startsWith(parent + ' / '); }
    function folderPathExists(parts) {
      if (!parts.length) return true;
      const candidate = pathString(parts);
      if (library.groups.some(group => pathString(normalized(group)) === candidate)) return true;
      return library.items.some(item => {
        const folder = pathString(normalized(item.folder || ''));
        return folder === candidate || isInside(folder, candidate);
      });
    }
    function repairCurrentPath() {
      while (currentPath.length && !folderPathExists(currentPath)) currentPath.pop();
    }
    function notify(message) {
      $('toast').textContent = message; $('toast').hidden = false;
      clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer = setTimeout(() => $('toast').hidden = true, 5000);
    }
    function escapeText(value) { return String(value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, ch => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch])); }

    async function refresh() {
      library = await api('/api/state');
      libraryLoaded = true;
      render();
      $('status').textContent = 'Connected to Archive Shelf.';
    }
    async function moveArchive(id, folder) {
      const item = library.items.find(entry => entry.id === id);
      if (!item || pathString(normalized(item.folder || '')) === pathString(folder)) return;
      try {
        await api('/api/move', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({id, folder:pathString(folder)})});
        await refresh();
        notify(folder.length ? `Moved “${item.name}” to ${pathString(folder)}.` : `Moved “${item.name}” to the top level.`);
      } catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
    }
    function enableArchiveDrop(target, destination) {
      const getDestination = typeof destination === 'function' ? destination : () => destination;
      target.addEventListener('dragover', event => {
        if (!event.dataTransfer || ![...event.dataTransfer.types].includes('application/x-archive-shelf-item')) return;
        event.preventDefault(); event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move'; target.classList.add('drop-target');
      });
      target.addEventListener('dragleave', event => {
        if (!target.contains(event.relatedTarget)) target.classList.remove('drop-target');
      });
      target.addEventListener('drop', async event => {
        const id = event.dataTransfer?.getData('application/x-archive-shelf-item');
        if (!id) return;
        event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); target.classList.remove('drop-target');
        await moveArchive(id, getDestination());
      });
    }
    function replayViewerUrl(item) {
      const viewer = new URL(`/replay-viewer.html`, `${location.protocol}//replay.localhost:${location.port}`);
      viewer.searchParams.set('id', item.id);
      viewer.searchParams.set('name', item.name);
      viewer.searchParams.set('parentOrigin', location.origin);
      if (replayState) viewer.searchParams.set('replay', replayState);
      if (replayState) viewer.hash = replayState;
      return viewer.href;
    }
    function mountArchive(item) {
      const mount = $('viewer-component');
      const replay = document.createElement('iframe');
      replay.src = replayViewerUrl(item);
      replay.title = `Archived site: ${item.name}`;
      replay.referrerPolicy = 'no-referrer';
      replay.allow = 'fullscreen';
      replay.allowFullscreen = true;
      replay.loading = 'eager';
      mount.replaceChildren(replay);
    }
    function displayCaptureTime(value) {
      const date = new Date(value);
      return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? String(value || 'Time unavailable') : date.toLocaleString();
    }
    async function loadSavedPages(archiveId) {
      const list = $('saved-pages-list');
      const status = $('pages-dialog-status');
      list.replaceChildren();
      status.textContent = 'Loading saved pages…';
      try {
        const result = await api('/api/archive-pages?id=' + encodeURIComponent(archiveId));
        if (archiveId !== openItemId) return;
        const pages = (result.pages || []).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.ts).getTime() - new Date(a.ts).getTime());
        status.textContent = pages.length + ' saved capture' + (pages.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '.';
        if (!pages.length) {
          const empty = document.createElement('div');
          empty.className = 'saved-pages-empty';
          empty.textContent = 'There are no saved pages in this archive.';
          list.append(empty);
          return;
        }
        for (const page of pages) {
          const row = document.createElement('div');
          row.className = 'saved-page-row';
          const info = document.createElement('div');
          info.className = 'saved-page-info';
          const title = document.createElement('div');
          title.className = 'saved-page-title';
          title.textContent = page.title || page.url || 'Untitled page';
          const meta = document.createElement('div');
          meta.className = 'saved-page-meta';
          meta.textContent = displayCaptureTime(page.ts) + ' · ' + page.url;
          info.append(title, meta);
          const remove = document.createElement('button');
          remove.className = 'saved-page-delete';
          remove.type = 'button';
          remove.textContent = 'Delete';
          remove.title = 'Delete this saved page capture from Archive Shelf';
          remove.onclick = async () => {
            const item = library.items.find(entry => entry.id === archiveId);
            const archiveName = item ? item.name : 'this archive';
            if (!confirm('Delete “' + (page.title || page.url) + '” captured ' + displayCaptureTime(page.ts) + ' from “' + archiveName + '”? This removes it from Archive Shelf’s saved copy; the original WACZ file is unchanged.')) return;
            remove.disabled = true;
            remove.textContent = 'Deleting…';
            status.textContent = 'Updating the archive… Larger WACZ files can take a little while.';
            try {
              await api('/api/archive-page?id=' + encodeURIComponent(archiveId) + '&page=' + encodeURIComponent(page.key), {method:'DELETE'});
              replayState = '';
              delete $('viewer-component').dataset.archiveId;
              writeRoute('replace');
              await refresh();
              await loadSavedPages(archiveId);
              notify('Saved page deleted from this archive.');
            } catch (error) {
              notify(error.message);
              await loadSavedPages(archiveId);
            }
          };
          row.append(info, remove);
          list.append(row);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        status.textContent = error.message;
      }
    }
    async function openSavedPages() {
      if (!openItemId) return;
      $('pages-dialog').showModal();
      await loadSavedPages(openItemId);
    }
    function render() {
      const oldFolder = pathString(currentPath);
      const oldArchiveId = openItemId;
      const oldReplayState = replayState;
      repairCurrentPath();
      const openItem = library.items.find(item => item.id === openItemId);
      if (openItemId && !openItem) { openItemId = null; replayState = ''; }
      if (!openItemId) replayState = '';
      if (oldFolder !== pathString(currentPath) || oldArchiveId !== openItemId || oldReplayState !== replayState) writeRoute('replace');
      const here = pathString(currentPath);
      $('folder-hint').textContent = here
        ? `A new folder will be created inside ${here}. Enter a child name, or a full path starting with ${here}.`
        : 'A folder will be created at the top level. Use / in the name to create nested folders.';
      $('groupPath').placeholder = currentPath.length ? 'e.g. Avatar the Last Airbender' : 'e.g. MTG, Sports, Instruments';
      $('drop-location').textContent = here ? `They’ll be added to ${here}.` : 'They’ll be added at the top level.';
      $('drop-zone').hidden = Boolean(openItemId);
      $('entries').hidden = Boolean(openItemId);
      $('viewer').hidden = !openItemId;
      if (openItemId && openItem) {
        $('viewer-title').textContent = openItem.name;
        $('viewer-manage-pages').hidden = !openItem.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.wacz');
        $('viewer-error').hidden = true;
        const mount = $('viewer-component');
        if (mount.dataset.archiveId !== openItem.id || mount.dataset.replayState !== replayState || !mount.querySelector('iframe')) {
          mount.dataset.archiveId = openItem.id;
          mount.dataset.replayState = replayState;
          mountArchive(openItem);
        }
      } else {
        $('viewer-component').replaceChildren();
        delete $('viewer-component').dataset.archiveId;
      }
      const crumbs = $('crumbs'); crumbs.replaceChildren();
      const up = document.createElement('button'); up.textContent = '← Parent folder'; up.disabled = currentPath.length === 0;
      up.title = currentPath.length ? 'Go up one folder' : 'You are already at the top level';
      up.onclick = () => navigate(currentPath.slice(0, -1));
      if (currentPath.length) enableArchiveDrop(up, currentPath.slice(0, -1));
      crumbs.append(up);
      const root = document.createElement('button'); root.textContent = 'All folders'; root.onclick = () => navigate([]);
      enableArchiveDrop(root, []);
      crumbs.append(root);
      currentPath.forEach((part, index) => {
        crumbs.append(document.createTextNode('›'));
        const current = document.createElement(index === currentPath.length - 1 ? 'strong' : 'button');
        current.textContent = part;
        if (current.tagName === 'BUTTON') current.onclick = () => navigate(currentPath.slice(0, index + 1));
        crumbs.append(current);
      });

      const parentPath = pathString(currentPath);
      const folders = new Set();
      for (const group of library.groups) {
        const parts = normalized(group);
        if (parts.length > currentPath.length && currentPath.every((part, i) => parts[i] === part)) folders.add(parts[currentPath.length]);
      }
      for (const item of library.items) {
        const parts = normalized(item.folder || '');
        if (parts.length > currentPath.length && currentPath.every((part, i) => parts[i] === part)) folders.add(parts[currentPath.length]);
      }

      const host = $('entries'); host.replaceChildren();
      for (const folderName of [...folders].sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
        const entry = document.createElement('div'); entry.className = 'entry';
        const main = document.createElement('div'); main.className = 'entry-main';
        const open = document.createElement('button'); open.className = 'entry-title folder-title'; open.textContent = '📁  ' + folderName; open.onclick = () => navigate([...currentPath, folderName]);
        const fullPath = pathString([...currentPath, folderName]);
        enableArchiveDrop(entry, normalized(fullPath));
        const count = library.items.filter(item => isInside(item.folder || '', fullPath)).length;
        const meta = document.createElement('div'); meta.className = 'entry-meta'; meta.textContent = count ? `${count} archive${count === 1 ? '' : 's'} inside` : 'Folder';
        main.append(open, meta);
        const del = document.createElement('button'); del.className = 'delete'; del.textContent = 'Delete folder'; del.title = `Delete folder ${fullPath}`;
        del.onclick = async () => {
          const contained = library.items.filter(item => isInside(item.folder || '', fullPath)).length;
          const note = contained ? ` Any archives inside will move up to ${parentPath || 'All folders'}; the archive files themselves will stay.` : '';
          if (!confirm(`Delete the folder “${fullPath}”?${note}`)) return;
          try { await api('/api/group?path=' + encodeURIComponent(fullPath), { method: 'DELETE' }); await refresh(); notify('Folder deleted.'); }
          catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
        };
        const move = document.createElement('button'); move.className = 'move'; move.textContent = 'Move'; move.title = `Move ${fullPath} and everything inside it`;
        move.onclick = async () => {
          const context = pathString(currentPath) || 'All folders';
          const destinationParent = prompt('Move folder into which existing folder? A short name is inside "' + context + '". Use "All folders / Name" for a top-level folder. Leave blank for the top level.');
          if (destinationParent === null) return;
          const canonicalParent = pathString(resolveMoveDestination(destinationParent, currentPath));
          try {
            const result = await api('/api/move-group', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({path:fullPath, parent:canonicalParent})});
            await refresh(); notify(`Moved “${folderName}” to ${result.folder || 'the top level'}.`);
          } catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
        };        const rename = document.createElement('button'); rename.className = 'move'; rename.textContent = 'Rename'; rename.title = `Rename ${fullPath} and everything inside it`;
        rename.onclick = async () => {
          const newName = prompt('Rename folder');
          if (newName === null || !newName.trim() || newName.trim() === folderName) return;
          try {
            const result = await api('/api/rename-group', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({path:fullPath, name:newName.trim()})});
            await refresh(); notify(`Renamed folder to ${result.folder}.`);
          } catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
        };
        entry.append(main, move, rename, del); host.append(entry);
      }

      const items = library.items.filter(item => pathString(normalized(item.folder || '')) === parentPath).sort((a,b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
      for (const item of items) {
        const entry = document.createElement('div'); entry.className = 'entry archive-entry'; entry.draggable = true;
        entry.addEventListener('dragstart', event => {
          event.dataTransfer?.setData('application/x-archive-shelf-item', item.id);
          if (event.dataTransfer) event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
        });
        entry.addEventListener('dragend', () => document.querySelectorAll('.drop-target').forEach(target => target.classList.remove('drop-target')));
        const main = document.createElement('div'); main.className = 'entry-main';
        const open = document.createElement('button'); open.className = 'entry-title'; open.textContent = '📄  ' + item.name; open.title = 'Open archive here in Archive Shelf'; open.onclick = () => { $('status').textContent = 'Archive opened inside Archive Shelf.'; navigate(currentPath, item.id); };
        const meta = document.createElement('div'); meta.className = 'entry-meta'; meta.textContent = item.size ? `${(item.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB · View archive here` : 'View archive here';
        main.append(open, meta);
        const move = document.createElement('button'); move.className = 'move'; move.textContent = 'Move'; move.title = 'Move this archive to another folder';
        move.onclick = async () => {
          const context = pathString(currentPath) || 'All folders';
          const destination = prompt('Move archive into which folder? A short name is inside "' + context + '". Use "All folders / Name" for a top-level folder. Leave blank for the top level.', item.folder || '');
          if (destination === null) return;
          const destinationFolder = resolveMoveDestination(destination, currentPath);
          const destinationPath = pathString(destinationFolder);
          try {
            await api('/api/move', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({id:item.id, folder:destinationPath})});
            await refresh(); notify(destinationPath ? `Archive moved to ${destinationPath}.` : 'Archive moved to the top level.');
          } catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
        };
        const del = document.createElement('button'); del.className = 'delete'; del.textContent = 'Remove'; del.title = 'Remove archive and its organizer copy';
        del.onclick = async () => {
          if (!confirm(`Remove “${item.name}” from Archive Shelf and delete the organizer’s local copy? Your original file will not be changed.`)) return;
          try { await api('/api/archive?id=' + encodeURIComponent(item.id), {method:'DELETE'}); await refresh(); notify('Archive removed from the organizer.'); }
          catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
        };
        entry.append(main, move, del); host.append(entry);
      }
      if (!host.children.length) {
        const empty = document.createElement('div'); empty.id = 'empty';
        empty.textContent = currentPath.length ? 'This folder is empty. Add an archive here or create a folder inside it.' : 'No folders yet. Create one on the left, then add your archive files.';
        host.append(empty);
      }
    }

    $('home-link').addEventListener('click', event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      window.location.assign('/');
    });
    $('viewer-home').onclick = () => navigate([]);
    function navigateReplay(direction) {
      const frame = $('viewer-component').querySelector('iframe');
      if (!frame?.contentWindow || !openItemId) return false;
      const expectedOrigin = `${location.protocol}//replay.localhost${location.port ? ':' + location.port : ''}`;
      frame.contentWindow.postMessage({ type: 'archivewebshelf-replay-navigation', id: openItemId, direction }, expectedOrigin);
      return true;
    }
    $('viewer-nav-back').onclick = () => navigate(currentPath);
    $('viewer-nav-forward').onclick = () => { if (!navigateReplay('forward')) history.forward(); };
    $('viewer-refresh').onclick = () => {
      const item = library.items.find(entry => entry.id === openItemId);
      if (item) mountArchive(item);
    };
    $('viewer-back').onclick = () => navigate(currentPath);
    $('viewer-manage-pages').onclick = openSavedPages;
    $('pages-dialog-close').onclick = () => $('pages-dialog').close();

    function applyRouteFromLocation() {
      const route = readRoute();
      currentPath = route.folder;
      openItemId = route.archiveId;
      replayState = route.replayState;
      if (libraryLoaded) render();
    }
    window.addEventListener('popstate', applyRouteFromLocation);
    window.addEventListener('message', event => {
      const mount = $('viewer-component');
      const frame = mount.querySelector('iframe');
      const expectedOrigin = `${location.protocol}//replay.localhost${location.port ? ':' + location.port : ''}`;
      const data = event.data;
      if (!frame || event.source !== frame.contentWindow || event.origin !== expectedOrigin ||
          !data || data.type !== 'archivewebshelf-replay-state' || data.id !== openItemId) return;
      const nextState = cleanReplayState(data.state || '');
      if (nextState === replayState) return;
      replayState = nextState;
      mount.dataset.replayState = replayState;
      // ReplayWeb.page keeps its own navigation inside the iframe. Mirror each
      // changed page into the Shelf history so browser Back/Forward and the
      // Shelf's page controls can restore the same archived location.
      writeRoute('push');
    });

    $('addGroup').onclick = async () => {
      const entered = normalized($('groupPath').value);
      const alreadyStartsAtCurrent = currentPath.length <= entered.length && currentPath.every((part, index) => part.toLocaleLowerCase() === entered[index].toLocaleLowerCase());
      const value = pathString(alreadyStartsAtCurrent ? entered : [...currentPath, ...entered]); if (!value) return;
      try { await api('/api/group', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({path:value})}); $('groupPath').value = ''; await refresh(); notify('Folder created.'); }
      catch (error) { notify(error.message); }
    };
    $('groupPath').addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Enter') $('addGroup').click(); });
    async function addFiles(fileList) {
      const files = [...fileList]; const folder = pathString(currentPath);
      if (!files.length) return;
      let added = 0;
      for (const file of files) {
        $('status').textContent = `Adding ${file.name}…`;
        try {
          await api('/api/import?name=' + encodeURIComponent(file.name) + '&folder=' + encodeURIComponent(folder), {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/octet-stream'}, body:file});
          added++;
        } catch (error) { notify(`${file.name}: ${error.message}`); }
      }
      $('status').textContent = added ? `${added} archive${added === 1 ? '' : 's'} added to ${folder || 'the top level'}.` : 'No archives were added.';
      await refresh();
    }
    $('files').onchange = async event => { const files = [...event.target.files]; event.target.value = ''; await addFiles(files); };

    const workspace = $('workspace');
    const dropZone = $('drop-zone');
    let dragDepth = 0;
    const isFileDrag = event => event.dataTransfer && [...event.dataTransfer.types].includes('Files');
    const isArchiveItemDrag = event => event.dataTransfer && [...event.dataTransfer.types].includes('application/x-archive-shelf-item');
    window.addEventListener('dragover', event => { if (isFileDrag(event)) event.preventDefault(); });
    window.addEventListener('drop', event => { if (isFileDrag(event)) event.preventDefault(); });
    workspace.addEventListener('dragenter', event => {
      if (!isFileDrag(event)) return;
      event.preventDefault(); dragDepth++; dropZone.classList.add('dragging');
    });
    workspace.addEventListener('dragover', event => {
      if (!isFileDrag(event)) return;
      event.preventDefault(); event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
    });
    workspace.addEventListener('dragleave', event => {
      if (!isFileDrag(event)) return;
      dragDepth = Math.max(0, dragDepth - 1);
      if (!dragDepth) dropZone.classList.remove('dragging');
    });
    workspace.addEventListener('drop', async event => {
      if (!isFileDrag(event)) return;
      event.preventDefault(); dragDepth = 0; dropZone.classList.remove('dragging');
      await addFiles(event.dataTransfer.files);
    });
    enableArchiveDrop(dropZone, () => [...currentPath]);

    async function migrateOldBrowserFiles() {
      const completeKey = 'archive-shelf-migrated-v3';
      if (localStorage.getItem(completeKey)) return;
      if (localStorage.getItem('archive-shelf-migrated-v2')) {
        localStorage.setItem(completeKey, 'yes');
        return;
      }
      let oldMeta;
      try { oldMeta = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('archive-shelf-meta') || '{"groups":[],"items":[]}'); }
      catch { oldMeta = {groups:[], items:[]}; }
      if (!oldMeta || !Array.isArray(oldMeta.groups) || !Array.isArray(oldMeta.items)) oldMeta = {groups:[], items:[]};
      if (!oldMeta.groups.length && !oldMeta.items.length) { localStorage.setItem(completeKey, 'yes'); return; }

      const request = indexedDB.open('archive-shelf', 1);
      const oldDb = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
        request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        request.onupgradeneeded = () => resolve(request.result);
      });
      if (!oldDb.objectStoreNames.contains('files')) { oldDb.close(); return; }
      const oldFile = id => new Promise((resolve,reject) => {
        const req = oldDb.transaction('files').objectStore('files').get(id);
        req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
        req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
      });
      try {
        for (const group of oldMeta.groups) {
          await api('/api/group', {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({path:group})});
        }
        let moved = 0;
        for (const item of oldMeta.items) {
          const migrationId = String(item.id || item.name || '');
          const itemKey = `archive-shelf-migrated-v3:${migrationId}`;
          if (localStorage.getItem(itemKey)) continue;
          const file = await oldFile(item.id);
          if (!file) { localStorage.setItem(itemKey, 'missing'); continue; }
          const original = file.name || item.name || 'archive.wacz';
          const query = new URLSearchParams({name: original, folder: item.path || '', migrationId});
          await api('/api/import?' + query, {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/octet-stream'},body:file});
          localStorage.setItem(itemKey, 'yes');
          moved++;
        }
        localStorage.setItem(completeKey, 'yes');
        if (moved) notify(`${moved} existing archive${moved === 1 ? '' : 's'} moved into the desktop-app organizer.`);
      } finally { oldDb.close(); }
    }

    const initialRoute = readRoute();
    currentPath = initialRoute.folder;
    openItemId = initialRoute.archiveId;
    replayState = initialRoute.replayState;
    $('status').textContent = 'Connecting to Archive Shelf…';
    refresh().then(migrateOldBrowserFiles).then(refresh).catch(error => { $('status').textContent = error.message; });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

replay-viewer.js - validates archive selection, starts replay, and forwards Back and Forward
================================================================================================
(() => {
  const message = document.getElementById("message");
  const replay = document.getElementById("replay");
  const query = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const id = query.get("id") || "";
  const name = query.get("name") || "";
  const requestedParentOrigin = query.get("parentOrigin") || "";

  function allowedParentOrigin(value) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(value);
      const samePort = url.port === location.port;
      const localHost = ["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "[::1]"].includes(url.hostname);
      return url.origin === value && ["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol) && samePort && localHost
        ? url.origin
        : "";
    } catch { return ""; }
  }

  const parentOrigin = allowedParentOrigin(requestedParentOrigin);
  const initialReplayState = query.get("replay") || location.hash.slice(1);
  if (initialReplayState && !location.hash) history.replaceState(history.state, "", `#${initialReplayState}`);

  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(id) || !name || name.includes("/") || name.includes("\\")) {
    replay.remove();
    message.hidden = false;
    message.textContent = "Archive Shelf could not find that archive. Return to the folder and open it again.";
    return;
  }

  replay.setAttribute("title", name);
  replay.setAttribute("source", `${location.origin}/library/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/${encodeURIComponent(name)}`);
  replay.setAttribute("deepLink", "");

  let lastReplayState = cleanState(location.hash.slice(1));
  function cleanState(value) {
    if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 12000) return "";
    const incoming = new URLSearchParams(value.startsWith("#") ? value.slice(1) : value);
    const result = new URLSearchParams();
    for (const key of ["url", "ts", "query", "view"]) if (incoming.has(key)) result.set(key, incoming.get(key) || "");
    return result.toString();
  }

  function tellShelf(state) {
    if (!parentOrigin) return;
    window.parent.postMessage({ type: "archivewebshelf-replay-state", id, state }, parentOrigin);
  }

  // The Shelf's Back/Forward controls live outside ReplayWeb.page. Forward
  // those actions to ReplayWeb's own toolbar so they use the history of the
  // archived site, rather than the organizer page's history.
  function navigateReplay(direction) {
    const replayAppFrame = replay.shadowRoot?.querySelector("iframe");
    const replayApp = replayAppFrame?.contentDocument?.querySelector("replay-app-main");
    const replayItem = replayApp?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("wr-item");
    const label = direction === "back" ? "Back" : "Forward";
    const control = replayItem?.shadowRoot?.querySelector(`a[aria-label="${label}"]`);
    if (!control) return false;
    control.click();
    return true;
  }

  window.addEventListener("message", event => {
    const data = event.data;
    if (event.source !== window.parent || event.origin !== parentOrigin ||
        !data || data.type !== "archivewebshelf-replay-navigation" || data.id !== id ||
        !["back", "forward"].includes(data.direction)) return;
    navigateReplay(data.direction);
  });

  replay.addEventListener("rwp-url-change", () => {
    const next = cleanState(location.hash.slice(1));
    if (next === lastReplayState) return;
    if (lastReplayState) {
      // ReplayWeb.page updates its own URL with replaceState. Preserve the prior
      // replay location before recording the new one so browser Back can return.
      history.replaceState(history.state, "", `#${lastReplayState}`);
      history.pushState(history.state, "", `#${next}`);
    } else {
      history.replaceState(history.state, "", next ? `#${next}` : location.pathname + location.search);
    }
    lastReplayState = next;
    tellShelf(next);
  });

  window.addEventListener("popstate", () => {
    lastReplayState = cleanState(location.hash.slice(1));
    tellShelf(lastReplayState);
  });

  if (lastReplayState) tellShelf(lastReplayState);
  replay.addEventListener("error", () => {
    message.hidden = false;
    message.textContent = "ReplayWeb.page could not open this archive. Check that the archive file is still in Archive Shelf, then try again.";
  });
})();

================================================================================================
check_replaywebpage_update.py - verified local ReplayWeb.page updater
================================================================================================
"""Check for and optionally install a verified ReplayWeb.page viewer release."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tarfile
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.error import URLError
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen


APP_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPLAY_DIR = APP_DIR / "replay"
VERSION_FILE = REPLAY_DIR / "version.txt"
REGISTRY_URL = "https://registry.npmjs.org/replaywebpage/latest"
UPDATE_FILES = {
    "ui.js": REPLAY_DIR / "ui.js",
    "sw.js": REPLAY_DIR / "sw.js",
    "LICENSE": REPLAY_DIR / "LICENSE.txt",
    "version.txt": VERSION_FILE,
}
VERSION_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$")
MAX_PACKAGE_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_ASSET_BYTES = 30 * 1024 * 1024


def version_key(version: str) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]:
    numbers = version.split("-", 1)[0].split(".")
    stable = 0 if "-" in version else 1
    return int(numbers[0]), int(numbers[1]), int(numbers[2]), stable


def get_installed_version() -> str | None:
    try:
        version = VERSION_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    except OSError:
        return None
    return version if VERSION_RE.fullmatch(version) else None


def get_latest_package() -> dict:
    request = Request(
        REGISTRY_URL,
        headers={"User-Agent": "Archive-Shelf-ReplayWebpage-Update-Checker/2.0"},
    )
    with urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
        metadata = json.loads(response.read(2 * 1024 * 1024 + 1).decode("utf-8"))
    version = metadata.get("version")
    dist = metadata.get("dist") or {}
    tarball = dist.get("tarball")
    integrity = dist.get("integrity")
    if not isinstance(version, str) or not VERSION_RE.fullmatch(version):
        raise ValueError("The package registry returned an unexpected version number.")
    if not isinstance(tarball, str) or not tarball.startswith("https://registry.npmjs.org/"):
        raise ValueError("The package registry returned an unexpected download address.")
    if not isinstance(integrity, str) or not integrity.startswith("sha512-"):
        raise ValueError("The package registry did not provide a SHA-512 package signature.")
    return {"version": version, "tarball": tarball, "integrity": integrity}


def download_package(package: dict) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    request = Request(
        package["tarball"],
        headers={"User-Agent": "Archive-Shelf-ReplayWebpage-Update-Checker/2.0"},
    )
    with urlopen(request, timeout=45) as response:
        archive_bytes = response.read(MAX_PACKAGE_BYTES + 1)
    if len(archive_bytes) > MAX_PACKAGE_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("The ReplayWeb.page package download is larger than expected.")

    expected = package["integrity"].removeprefix("sha512-")
    actual = base64.b64encode(hashlib.sha512(archive_bytes).digest()).decode("ascii")
    if not hmac.compare_digest(expected, actual):
        raise ValueError("The downloaded package did not match the registry's SHA-512 signature.")

    wanted = {"package/package.json", "package/ui.js", "package/sw.js", "package/LICENSE"}
    extracted: dict[str, bytes] = {}
    try:
        with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(archive_bytes), mode="r:gz") as archive:
            for member in archive.getmembers():
                if member.name not in wanted:
                    continue
                if not member.isfile() or member.size < 0 or member.size > MAX_ASSET_BYTES:
                    raise ValueError(f"The package contains an invalid {member.name} file.")
                stream = archive.extractfile(member)
                if stream is None:
                    raise ValueError(f"The package is missing {member.name}.")
                extracted[member.name] = stream.read(MAX_ASSET_BYTES + 1)
    except (tarfile.TarError, OSError) as error:
        raise ValueError("The downloaded ReplayWeb.page package could not be opened.") from error

    if set(extracted) != wanted:
        missing = ", ".join(sorted(wanted - set(extracted)))
        raise ValueError(f"The package is missing required viewer files: {missing}.")
    package_info = json.loads(extracted["package/package.json"].decode("utf-8"))
    if package_info.get("name") != "replaywebpage" or package_info.get("version") != package["version"]:
        raise ValueError("The downloaded viewer version did not match the registry information.")

    return {
        "ui.js": extracted["package/ui.js"],
        "sw.js": extracted["package/sw.js"],
        "LICENSE.txt": extracted["package/LICENSE"],
        "version.txt": (package["version"] + "\n").encode("utf-8"),
    }


def ask_to_update(version: str) -> bool:
    try:
        answer = input(f"Download and install ReplayWeb.page {version}? [y/N] ").strip().lower()
    except EOFError:
        return False
    return answer in {"y", "yes"}


def install_package(version: str, updated: dict[str, bytes]) -> Path:
    originals = {name: path.read_bytes() for name, path in UPDATE_FILES.items() if path.exists()}
    missing = set(UPDATE_FILES) - set(originals)
    if missing:
        raise ValueError("The local viewer files are incomplete: " + ", ".join(sorted(missing)))

    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    backup_dir = APP_DIR / "backups" / f"replaywebpage-{version}-{stamp}"
    backup_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for name, path in UPDATE_FILES.items():
        shutil.copy2(path, backup_dir / name)

    temp_paths: dict[str, Path] = {}
    replaced: list[str] = []
    try:
        for name, path in UPDATE_FILES.items():
            temporary = path.with_name(f".{path.name}.{uuid.uuid4().hex}.update-tmp")
            temporary.write_bytes(updated[name])
            temp_paths[name] = temporary
        for name, path in UPDATE_FILES.items():
            temp_paths[name].replace(path)
            replaced.append(name)
    except OSError:
        for name in replaced:
            UPDATE_FILES[name].write_bytes(originals[name])
        raise
    finally:
        for temporary in temp_paths.values():
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)

    return backup_dir


def main() -> None:
    print("Archive Shelf — ReplayWeb.page update check")
    print("This checks the viewer package and asks before replacing its local files.\n")

    current = get_installed_version()
    if not current:
        print("I couldn't read replay/version.txt. The local viewer may be incomplete.")
        return

    print(f"Archive Shelf viewer: {current}")
    print(f"Archive Shelf replay worker: {current}")

    try:
        latest_package = get_latest_package()
        latest = latest_package["version"]
    except (OSError, URLError, TimeoutError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as error:
        print("I couldn't check the latest version online.")
        print(f"Details: {error}")
        print("Try again when the computer has an internet connection.")
        return

    print(f"Latest stable ReplayWeb.page package: {latest}")
    if version_key(latest) == version_key(current):
        print("You're up to date.")
        return
    if version_key(latest) < version_key(current):
        print("The installed viewer is newer than the registry's latest stable version. No downgrade was made.")
        return
    if not ask_to_update(latest):
        print("No files were changed.")
        return

    try:
        updated = download_package(latest_package)
        backup_dir = install_package(latest, updated)
    except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as error:
        print("The update could not be completed. Your archives were not changed.")
        print(f"Details: {error}")
        return

    print(f"Installed ReplayWeb.page {latest} locally.")
    print(f"Backup copies are in: {backup_dir}")
    print("Close and reopen Archive Shelf to load the updated viewer.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import "../globals";

import type { BrowserRecorder } from "./browser-recorder";

declare global {
  interface Window {
    recorders: Record<string, BrowserRecorder>;
    newRecId: string | null;
    shelfTargets: Record<string, { baseUrl: string; folder: string; title: string }>;

================================================================================================
browser-extension/archiveweb-page-source/src/ext/bg.ts - recorder background and WACZ transfer
================================================================================================
import { BrowserRecorder } from "./browser-recorder";
import { Downloader } from "../sw/downloader";

import { CollectionLoader } from "@webrecorder/wabac/swlib";

import { listAllMsg } from "../utils";

import {
  getLocalOption,
  removeLocalOption,
  setLocalOption,
} from "../localstorage";

// ===========================================================================
self.recorders = {};
self.newRecId = null;
self.shelfTargets = {};

// @ts-expect-error - TS7034 - Variable 'newRecUrl' implicitly has type 'any' in some locations where its type cannot be determined.
let newRecUrl = null;
// @ts-expect-error - TS7034 - Variable 'newRecCollId' implicitly has type 'any' in some locations where its type cannot be determined.
let newRecCollId = null;

// @ts-expect-error - TS7034 - Variable 'defaultCollId' implicitly has type 'any' in some locations where its type cannot be determined.
let defaultCollId = null;
let autorun = false;

const openWinMap = new Map();

const collLoader = new CollectionLoader();

const disabledCSPTabs = new Set();
// ===========================================================================

function main() {
  chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: "#4d7c0f" });

  chrome.contextMenus.create({
    id: "toggle-rec",
    title: "Start Recording",
    contexts: ["browser_action"],
  });
  chrome.contextMenus.create({
    id: "view-rec",
    title: "View Web Archives",
    contexts: ["all"],
  });
}

// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'port' implicitly has an 'any' type.
chrome.runtime.onConnect.addListener((port) => {
  switch (port.name) {
    case "popup-port":
      popupHandler(port);
      break;
  }
});

// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'port' implicitly has an 'any' type.
function popupHandler(port) {
  if (
    !port.sender ||
    (port.sender.url !== chrome.runtime.getURL("popup.html") &&
      port.sender.url !== chrome.runtime.getURL("shelf-popup.html"))
  ) {
    return;
  }

  let tabId: number | null = null;

  // @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'message' implicitly has an 'any' type.
  port.onMessage.addListener(async (message) => {
    switch (message.type) {
      case "startUpdates":
        if (!Number.isInteger(message.tabId)) break;
        tabId = Number(message.tabId);
        if (self.recorders[tabId]) {
          const recorder = self.recorders[tabId] as BrowserRecorder & { port?: TODOFixMe };
          recorder.port = port;
          recorder.doUpdateStatus();
        }
        port.postMessage(await listAllMsg(collLoader));
        break;

      case "startRecording": {
        const { collId, autorun } = message;
        if (tabId === null) break;
        if (message.shelf) {
          self.shelfTargets[collId] = message.shelf;
          await chrome.storage.local.set({ [`archiveShelfTarget:${collId}`]: message.shelf });
        }
        const err = await startRecorder(tabId, { collId, port, autorun }, message.url);
        if (err) {
          port.postMessage({
            type: "status",
            recording: false,
            collId,
            failureMsg: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
          });
        }
        break;
      }

      case "stopShelfSession":
        saveShelfCollection(message.collId, port);
        break;

      case "stopRecording":
        if (tabId !== null) stopRecorder(tabId);
        break;

      case "toggleBehaviors":
        if (tabId !== null) toggleBehaviors(tabId);
        break;

      case "newColl": {
        const { name } = await collLoader.initNewColl({ title: message.title });
        defaultCollId = name;
        port.postMessage(await listAllMsg(collLoader, { defaultCollId }));
        await setLocalOption("defaultCollId", defaultCollId);
        break;
      }
    }
  });

  port.onDisconnect.addListener(() => {
    const disconnectedTabId = tabId;
    if (disconnectedTabId !== null && self.recorders[disconnectedTabId]) {
      const recorder = self.recorders[disconnectedTabId] as BrowserRecorder & { port?: TODOFixMe };
      recorder.port = null;
    }
  });
}

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tab' implicitly has an 'any' type. | TS7006 - Parameter 'reason' implicitly has an 'any' type.
chrome.debugger.onDetach.addListener((tab, reason) => {
  // target closed, delete recorder as this tab will not be used again
  if (reason === "target_closed") {
    delete self.recorders[tab.id];
  }
});

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tab' implicitly has an 'any' type.
chrome.tabs.onCreated.addListener((tab) => {
  if (!tab.id) {
    return;
  }

  let openUrl = null;
  let start = false;
  let waitForTabUpdate = true;
  let collId = null;

  // start recording from extension in new tab use case
  // @ts-expect-error - TS7005 - Variable 'newRecUrl' implicitly has an 'any' type.
  if (newRecUrl && tab.pendingUrl === "about:blank") {
    start = true;
    openUrl = newRecUrl;
    // @ts-expect-error - TS7005 - Variable 'newRecCollId' implicitly has an 'any' type. | TS7005 - Variable 'defaultCollId' implicitly has an 'any' type.
    collId = newRecCollId || defaultCollId;
    newRecUrl = null;
    newRecCollId = null;
  } else if (
    tab.openerTabId &&
    (!tab.pendingUrl || isValidUrl(tab.pendingUrl)) &&
    // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'running' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
    self.recorders[tab.openerTabId]?.running
  ) {
    // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'collId' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
    collId = self.recorders[tab.openerTabId].collId;

    start = true;
    if (tab.pendingUrl) {
      waitForTabUpdate = false;
      openUrl = tab.pendingUrl;
    }
  }

  if (start) {
    if (openUrl && !isValidUrl(openUrl)) {
      return;
    }
    startRecorder(
      tab.id,
      { waitForTabUpdate, collId, openUrl, autorun },
      openUrl,
    );
  }
});

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tabId' implicitly has an 'any' type. | TS7006 - Parameter 'changeInfo' implicitly has an 'any' type.
chrome.tabs.onUpdated.addListener((tabId, changeInfo) => {
  if (tabId && self.recorders[tabId]) {
    const recorder = self.recorders[tabId];
    if (changeInfo.url) {
      // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'failureMsg' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
      recorder.failureMsg = null;
    }

    if (changeInfo.url && openWinMap.has(changeInfo.url)) {
      openWinMap.delete(changeInfo.url);
    }

    // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'waitForTabUpdate' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
    if (recorder.waitForTabUpdate) {
      if (isValidUrl(changeInfo.url)) {
        recorder.attach();
      } else {
        // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'waitForTabUpdate' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
        recorder.waitForTabUpdate = false;
        delete self.recorders[tabId];
        return;
      }
    }
  } else if (changeInfo.url && openWinMap.has(changeInfo.url)) {
    const collId = openWinMap.get(changeInfo.url);
    openWinMap.delete(changeInfo.url);
    if (!tabId || !isValidUrl(changeInfo.url)) {
      return;
    }
    startRecorder(tabId, { collId, autorun }, changeInfo.url);
  }
});

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tabId' implicitly has an 'any' type.
chrome.tabs.onRemoved.addListener((tabId) => {
  delete self.recorders[tabId];
  removeLocalOption(`${tabId}-collId`);
});

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'info' implicitly has an 'any' type. | TS7006 - Parameter 'tab' implicitly has an 'any' type.
chrome.contextMenus.onClicked.addListener((info, tab) => {
  switch (info.menuItemId) {
    case "view-rec":
      chrome.tabs.create({ url: chrome.runtime.getURL("index.html") });
      break;

    case "toggle-rec":
      if (!isRecording(tab.id)) {
        if (isValidUrl(tab.url)) {
          startRecorder(tab.id);
        }
      } else {
        stopRecorder(tab.id);
      }
      break;
  }
});

// ===========================================================================
async function startRecorder(tabId: number, opts: TODOFixMe = {}, openUrl?: string) {
  if (openUrl && !opts.openUrl) opts.openUrl = openUrl;

  const previous = self.recorders[tabId] as (BrowserRecorder & {
    collId?: string;
    running?: boolean;
    isAttached?: boolean;
  }) | undefined;
  if (previous && opts.collId && previous.collId !== opts.collId) {
    // A new capture always gets a fresh recorder. Reusing one can retain the
    // previous session's URL and collection, causing the wrong page to be
    // recorded after a failed or unfinished session.
    if (previous.running || previous.isAttached) await previous.detach();
    delete self.recorders[tabId];
  }

  if (!self.recorders[tabId]) {
    opts.collLoader = collLoader;
    opts.openWinMap = openWinMap;
    self.recorders[tabId] = new BrowserRecorder({ tabId }, opts);
  } else {
    self.recorders[tabId].setAutoRunBehavior(opts.autorun);
    (self.recorders[tabId] as BrowserRecorder & { port?: TODOFixMe }).port = opts.port;
  }

  let err = null;
  const { waitForTabUpdate } = opts;

  // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'running' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
  if (!waitForTabUpdate && !self.recorders[tabId].running) {
    try {
      self.recorders[tabId].setCollId(opts.collId);
      await self.recorders[tabId].attach();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn(e);
      err = e;
    }
    return err;
  }
}
// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tabId' implicitly has an 'any' type.
function stopRecorder(tabId) {
  if (self.recorders[tabId]) {
    self.recorders[tabId].detach();
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

// Build a WACZ from the recorder's saved session, then stream it into the local Shelf.
async function saveShelfCollection(collId: string, port: TODOFixMe) {
  let target = self.shelfTargets[collId];
  if (!target) {
    const stored = await chrome.storage.local.get(`archiveShelfTarget:${collId}`);
    target = stored[`archiveShelfTarget:${collId}`];
  }
  if (!target) {
    return reportShelfResult(port, { ok: false, error: "This recording has no Archive Shelf destination." });
  }

  try {
    const recorders = (Object.values(self.recorders) as unknown as Array<{
      collId: string;
      running: boolean;
      detach: () => Promise<void>;
    }>).filter(
      (rec) => rec.collId === collId && rec.running,
    );
    // Recorder tabs share a debugger session; detach them one at a time so the
    // final recorder can fully detach the browser debugger.
    for (const recorder of recorders) await recorder.detach();

    const coll = await collLoader.loadColl(collId);
    if (!coll) throw new Error("The recording session could not be found.");

    const safeBaseName = String(target.title || "Archived Site")
      .normalize("NFKC")
      .replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*\x00-\x1f]/g, "-")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[. ]+$/g, "")
      .slice(0, 110) || "Archived Site";
    const filename = `${safeBaseName}.wacz`;
    const download = new Downloader({
      coll,
      format: "wacz",
      filename,
      softwareString: "Archive Shelf recording, using Webrecorder ArchiveWeb.page",
    });
    reportShelfProgress(port, "Building the WACZ file…");
    let response: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof download.download>>;
    try {
      response = await download.download();
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error("WACZ creation failed: " + (error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
    }
    if (!(response instanceof Response) || !response.body) {
      throw new Error("WACZ creation failed: ArchiveWeb.page returned an empty file.");
    }

    let uploadBody: Blob;
    reportShelfProgress(port, "Preparing the WACZ upload…");
    try {
      uploadBody = await response.blob();
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error("WACZ upload preparation failed: " + (error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
    }
    if (!uploadBody.size) {
      throw new Error("WACZ creation failed: ArchiveWeb.page returned an empty file.");
    }

    const destination = new URL("/api/import", target.baseUrl);
    destination.searchParams.set("name", filename);
    destination.searchParams.set("folder", target.folder || "");
    const uploadOptions: RequestInit = {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream" },
      body: uploadBody,
    };
    reportShelfProgress(port, "Uploading the WACZ to Archive Shelf (" + target.baseUrl + ")…");
    let upload: Response;
    try {
      upload = await fetch(destination.href, uploadOptions);
    } catch (error) {
      const details = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      throw new Error("Upload to Archive Shelf failed at " + target.baseUrl + ". Keep Archive Shelf running and check Brave's localhost access for the recorder. Browser detail: " + details);
    }
    let result: { error?: string } = {};
    try {
      result = await upload.json();
    } catch (_error) {
      // The organizer error below is enough if it did not return JSON.
    }
    if (!upload.ok) throw new Error(result.error || `Archive Shelf returned ${upload.status}.`);

    const saved = {
      ok: true,
      name: filename,
      folder: target.folder || "",
      savedAt: Date.now(),
    };
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ archiveShelfLastSave: saved });
    await chrome.storage.local.remove(`archiveShelfTarget:${collId}`);
    chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: "✓" });
    chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: "#4d7c0f" });
    delete self.shelfTargets[collId];
    reportShelfResult(port, saved);
  } catch (error) {
    const failure = {
      ok: false,
      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
      savedAt: Date.now(),
    };
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ archiveShelfLastSave: failure });
    chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: "!" });
    chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: "#a12a36" });
    reportShelfResult(port, failure);
  }
}

function reportShelfProgress(port: TODOFixMe, message: string) {
  try {
    port.postMessage({ type: "shelfExportProgress", message });
  } catch (_error) {
    // The popup can close while a large archive is being saved.
  }
}

function reportShelfResult(port: TODOFixMe, result: { ok: boolean; [key: string]: unknown }) {
  try {
    port.postMessage({ type: "shelfExportResult", ...result });
  } catch (_error) {
    // The popup can close while a large archive is being saved.
  }
}
// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tabId' implicitly has an 'any' type.
function toggleBehaviors(tabId) {
  if (self.recorders[tabId]) {
    self.recorders[tabId].toggleBehaviors();
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tabId' implicitly has an 'any' type.
function isRecording(tabId) {
  // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'running' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
  return self.recorders[tabId]?.running;
}

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'url' implicitly has an 'any' type.
function isValidUrl(url) {
  return (
    url &&
    (url === "about:blank" ||
      url.startsWith("https:") ||
      url.startsWith("http:"))
  );
}

// ===========================================================================
chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener(
  // @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameters 'message' and 'sender' implicitly have an 'any' type.
  async (message, sender) => {
    if (sender?.id !== chrome.runtime.id || !message || typeof message !== "object") {
      return;
    }

    let senderOrigin = "";
    try {
      senderOrigin = new URL(sender.url).origin;
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    if (senderOrigin !== new URL(chrome.runtime.getURL("/")).origin) {
      return;
    }

    switch (message.msg) {
      case "startNew":
        if (
          typeof message.url !== "string" ||
          !/^https?:\/\//i.test(message.url) ||
          !isValidUrl(message.url) ||
          typeof message.collId !== "string" ||
          !message.collId ||
          message.collId.length > 200
        ) {
          return;
        }
        newRecUrl = message.url;
        newRecCollId = message.collId;
        autorun = Boolean(message.autorun);
        defaultCollId = await getLocalOption("defaultCollId");
        chrome.tabs.create({ url: "about:blank" });
        break;

      case "disableCSP":
        if (!Number.isInteger(sender.tab?.id) || message.tabId !== sender.tab.id) {
          return;
        }
        void disableCSPForTab(sender.tab.id);
        break;
    }
  },
);

// ===========================================================================
// @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'tabId' implicitly has an 'any' type.
async function disableCSPForTab(tabId) {
  if (disabledCSPTabs.has(tabId)) {
    return;
  }

  await new Promise((resolve) => {
    chrome.debugger.attach({ tabId }, "1.3", () => {
      // @ts-expect-error - TS2794 - Expected 1 arguments, but got 0. Did you forget to include 'void' in your type argument to 'Promise'?
      resolve();
    });
  });

  await new Promise((resolve) => {
    chrome.debugger.sendCommand(
      { tabId },
      "Page.setBypassCSP",
      { enabled: true },
      // @ts-expect-error - TS7006 - Parameter 'resp' implicitly has an 'any' type.
      (resp) => resolve(resp),
    );
  });

  disabledCSPTabs.add(tabId);

  // hacky: don't detach if any recorders are running, otherwise will disconnect
  for (const rec of Object.values(self.recorders)) {
    // @ts-expect-error - TS2339 - Property 'running' does not exist on type 'BrowserRecorder'.
    if (rec.running) {
      return;
    }
  }

  await new Promise((resolve) => {
    chrome.debugger.detach({ tabId }, () => {
      // @ts-expect-error - TS2794 - Expected 1 arguments, but got 0. Did you forget to include 'void' in your type argument to 'Promise'?
      resolve();
    });
  });
}

// ===========================================================================
chrome.runtime.onInstalled.addListener(main);

if (self.importScripts) {
  self.importScripts("sw.js");
}

    #import-panel summary { cursor: pointer; color: #315fcb; }
    #import-file { display: block; width: 100%; margin-top: 8px; padding: 6px; border: 1px solid #cbd3df; border-radius: 6px; background: white; font-size: 12px; }
    #import-archive { width: 100%; margin-top: 7px; padding: 8px; font-size: 12px; }
  </style>
</head>

browser-extension/archiveweb-page-source/static/shelf-popup.js - save-folder picker and recording controls
================================================================================================
const START_PORT = 8765;
const PORT_COUNT = 30;
const SHELF_DRIVE = "application/x-archive-shelf-item";

const selectedFolder = document.getElementById("selected-folder");
const chooseFolderButton = document.getElementById("choose-folder");
const recordButton = document.getElementById("record");
const statusBox = document.getElementById("status");
const folderDialog = document.getElementById("folder-dialog");
const folderList = document.getElementById("folder-list");
const folderCrumbs = document.getElementById("folder-crumbs");
const importInput = document.getElementById("import-file");
const importButton = document.getElementById("import-archive");

let shelfBase = null;
let shelfState = { groups: [], items: [] };
let destination = "";
let pickerPath = [];
let recorderPort = null;
let activeTab = null;
let isStarting = false;
let isRecording = false;
let isSaving = false;
let isImporting = false;
let currentCollectionId = "";
let pendingCollection = null;
let startTimer = null;

function setStatus(message, isError = false) {
  statusBox.textContent = message;
  statusBox.classList.toggle("error", isError);
}

function normalizePath(value) {
  return String(value || "").split(/[\\/]/).map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

function pathText(parts) {
  return parts.join(" / ");
}

function isShelfState(state) {
  return state && state.application === "archive-shelf" && state.protocol === 1 && Array.isArray(state.groups) && Array.isArray(state.items);
}

async function findShelf() {
  const attempts = Array.from({ length: PORT_COUNT }, async (_, index) => {
    const port = START_PORT + index;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 700);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/state`, {
        cache: "no-store",
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("No Shelf server on that port");
      const state = await response.json();
      if (!isShelfState(state)) throw new Error("Not Archive Shelf");
      return { base: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`, state };
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  });
  try {
    return await Promise.any(attempts);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function allFolderPaths() {
  const paths = new Set(shelfState.groups.map((path) => pathText(normalizePath(path))));
  for (const item of shelfState.items) {
    const folder = pathText(normalizePath(item.folder));
    if (folder) paths.add(folder);
    const parts = normalizePath(folder);
    for (let index = 1; index < parts.length; index++) paths.add(pathText(parts.slice(0, index)));
  }
  return [...paths];
}

function directFolders(parent) {
  const parentText = pathText(parent);
  const children = new Set();
  for (const fullPath of allFolderPaths()) {
    const parts = normalizePath(fullPath);
    if (parts.length > parent.length && parent.every((part, index) => parts[index] === part)) {
      const child = parts[parent.length];
      if (child) children.add(child);
    }
  }
  for (const item of shelfState.items) {
    const parts = normalizePath(item.folder);
    if (parts.length > parent.length && parent.every((part, index) => parts[index] === part)) {
      const child = parts[parent.length];
      if (child) children.add(child);
    }
  }
  return [...children].sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));
}

function setDestination(path) {
  destination = pathText(path);
  selectedFolder.textContent = destination || "All folders (top level)";
  folderDialog.hidden = true;
  updateButton();
}

function renderFolderPicker() {
  folderCrumbs.replaceChildren();
  const addCrumb = (label, path) => {
    const button = document.createElement("button");
    button.type = "button";
    button.textContent = label;
    button.addEventListener("click", () => {
      pickerPath = path;
      renderFolderPicker();
    });
    folderCrumbs.append(button);
  };
  addCrumb("All folders", []);
  pickerPath.forEach((part, index) => {
    folderCrumbs.append(document.createTextNode("›"));
    addCrumb(part, pickerPath.slice(0, index + 1));
  });

  folderList.replaceChildren();
  const children = directFolders(pickerPath);
  if (!children.length) {
    const empty = document.createElement("div");
    empty.className = "folder-empty";
    empty.textContent = pickerPath.length ? "No subfolders here." : "No folders yet. The archive will be saved at the top level.";
    folderList.append(empty);
  }
  for (const name of children) {
    const button = document.createElement("button");
    button.type = "button";
    button.className = "folder-option";
    const icon = document.createElement("span");
    icon.className = "folder-icon";
    icon.textContent = "📁";
    const label = document.createElement("span");
    label.className = "folder-name";
    label.textContent = name || "(unnamed folder)";
    const chevron = document.createElement("span");
    chevron.className = "folder-chevron";
    chevron.textContent = "›";
    button.append(icon, label, chevron);
    button.addEventListener("click", () => {
      pickerPath = [...pickerPath, name];
      renderFolderPicker();
    });
    folderList.append(button);
  }
}

function updateButton() {
  recordButton.disabled = !shelfBase || !activeTab || isStarting || isSaving || isImporting;
  recordButton.textContent = isSaving ? "Saving WACZ…" : isRecording ? "Stop and Save WACZ" : isStarting ? "Starting…" : "Start Archiving";
  chooseFolderButton.disabled = !shelfBase || isRecording || isSaving || isImporting;
  importButton.disabled = !shelfBase || !importInput.files.length || isRecording || isSaving || isImporting;
}

async function importExistingArchive() {
  const file = importInput.files[0];
  if (!file || !shelfBase) return;
  isImporting = true;
  updateButton();
  setStatus(`Importing ${file.name} into ${destination || "All folders"}…`);
  const query = new URLSearchParams({ name: file.name, folder: destination });
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${shelfBase}/api/import?${query}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream" },
      body: file,
    });
    let result = {};
    try { result = await response.json(); } catch (_error) { /* use a clear generic error below */ }
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || `Archive Shelf returned ${response.status}.`);
    importInput.value = "";
    setStatus(`Imported ${file.name} into ${destination || "All folders (top level)"}.`);
    const refreshed = await findShelf();
    if (refreshed) shelfState = refreshed.state;
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus(error.message || "Could not import the archive into Archive Shelf.", true);
  } finally {
    isImporting = false;
    updateButton();
  }
}

async function refreshShelf() {
  setStatus("Looking for Archive Shelf…");
  recordButton.disabled = true;
  const result = await findShelf();
  if (!result) {
    shelfBase = null;
    chooseFolderButton.disabled = true;
    selectedFolder.textContent = "Archive Shelf is not running";
    setStatus("Start Archive Shelf, then refresh folders.", true);
    updateButton();
    return;
  }
  shelfBase = result.base;
  shelfState = result.state;
  setStatus("Choose where to save, then start archiving.");
  updateButton();
}

function listenForNextCollection() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      pendingCollection = null;
      reject(new Error("ArchiveWeb.page did not create the new recording session."));
    }, 10000);
    pendingCollection = (message) => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      pendingCollection = null;
      if (!message.collId) reject(new Error("Could not identify the new recording session."));
      else resolve(message.collId);
    };
  });
}

function onRecorderMessage(message) {
  if (message.type === "shelfExportProgress") {
    setStatus(message.message || "Saving WACZ to Archive Shelf…");
    return;
  }
  if (message.type === "collections" && pendingCollection) {
    pendingCollection(message);
    return;
  }
  if (message.type === "status") {
    if (isSaving && !isStarting && !message.failureMsg) return;
    const wasStarting = isStarting;
    if (message.failureMsg || message.recording) {
      isStarting = false;
      clearTimeout(startTimer);
      startTimer = null;
    }
    if (message.collId) currentCollectionId = message.collId;
    isRecording = Boolean(message.recording);
    if (message.failureMsg) setStatus(`Recorder error: ${message.failureMsg}`, true);
    else if (isRecording) {
      const pages = message.numPages || 0;
      const pending = message.numPending || 0;
      setStatus(`Recording ${pages} page${pages === 1 ? "" : "s"}${pending ? ` · ${pending} items still loading` : ""}.`);
    } else if (wasStarting) setStatus("Waiting for the recorder to start…");
    updateButton();
  }
  if (message.type === "shelfExportResult") {
    isSaving = false;
    isRecording = false;
    if (message.ok) {
      setStatus(`Saved ${message.name} to ${message.folder || "All folders (top level)"}.`);
    } else {
      setStatus(message.error || "The recording could not be saved to Archive Shelf.", true);
    }
    updateButton();
  }
}

async function connectRecorder(forceReconnect = false) {
  if (forceReconnect && recorderPort) {
    const oldPort = recorderPort;
    recorderPort = null;
    oldPort.disconnect();
  }
  const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
  activeTab = tabs[0] || null;
  if (!activeTab || !activeTab.id || !/^https?:/.test(activeTab.url || "")) {
    setStatus("Open a regular website tab before starting an archive.", true);
    updateButton();
    return;
  }
  const port = chrome.runtime.connect({ name: "popup-port" });
  recorderPort = port;
  port.onMessage.addListener(onRecorderMessage);
  port.onDisconnect.addListener(() => {
    if (recorderPort === port) {
      recorderPort = null;
      if (!isSaving) setStatus("Recorder connection closed. Try starting again to reconnect.", true);
      updateButton();
    }
  });
  try {
    port.postMessage({ type: "startUpdates", tabId: activeTab.id });
  } catch (error) {
    if (recorderPort === port) recorderPort = null;
    setStatus(error.message || "Could not connect to the recorder.", true);
  }
  updateButton();
}

async function startRecording() {
  if (!shelfBase) return;
  isSaving = true;
  isStarting = false;
  updateButton();
  setStatus("Preparing a new recording…");
  try {
    await connectRecorder(true);
    if (!recorderPort || !activeTab) throw new Error("Could not connect to the recorder. Try reopening the extension popup.");
    const tabTitle = activeTab.title || new URL(activeTab.url).hostname;
    const collIdPromise = listenForNextCollection();
    recorderPort.postMessage({ type: "newColl", title: tabTitle });
    currentCollectionId = await collIdPromise;
    recorderPort.postMessage({
      type: "startRecording",
      collId: currentCollectionId,
      url: activeTab.url,
      autorun: false,
      shelf: { baseUrl: shelfBase, folder: destination, title: tabTitle },
    });
    isSaving = false;
    isStarting = true;
    setStatus("Waiting for the recorder to start…");
    startTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!isStarting) return;
      isStarting = false;
      setStatus("The recorder did not confirm that it started. Try again.", true);
      updateButton();
    }, 15000);
  } catch (error) {
    isSaving = false;
    isStarting = false;
    clearTimeout(startTimer);
    startTimer = null;
    setStatus(error.message || "Could not start the recorder.", true);
  }
  updateButton();
}

function stopAndSave() {
  if (!currentCollectionId) return;
  isRecording = false;
  isSaving = true;
  updateButton();
  setStatus("Finishing the recording, then saving its WACZ to Archive Shelf…");
  if (!recorderPort) {
    connectRecorder(true).then(() => {
      if (!recorderPort) throw new Error("Recorder connection could not be restored.");
      recorderPort.postMessage({ type: "stopShelfSession", collId: currentCollectionId });
    }).catch((error) => {
      isSaving = false;
      setStatus(error.message || "Could not reconnect to save the WACZ.", true);
      updateButton();
    });
    return;
  }
  try {
    recorderPort.postMessage({ type: "stopShelfSession", collId: currentCollectionId });
  } catch (error) {
    recorderPort = null;
    isSaving = false;
    setStatus(error.message || "Recorder connection closed before saving.", true);
    updateButton();
  }
}

chooseFolderButton.addEventListener("click", async () => {
  const result = await findShelf();
  if (!result) {
    setStatus("Start Archive Shelf, then refresh folders.", true);
    return;
  }
  shelfBase = result.base;
  shelfState = result.state;
  pickerPath = normalizePath(destination);
  folderDialog.hidden = false;
  renderFolderPicker();
});
document.getElementById("close-folders").addEventListener("click", () => { folderDialog.hidden = true; });
document.getElementById("use-folder").addEventListener("click", () => setDestination(pickerPath));
document.getElementById("refresh").addEventListener("click", refreshShelf);
recordButton.addEventListener("click", () => isRecording ? stopAndSave() : startRecording());
importInput.addEventListener("change", updateButton);
importButton.addEventListener("click", importExistingArchive);

refreshShelf().then(connectRecorder);
